Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sat Nov 01 2014 02:33:01 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

We should have narcotest in public, and Jury Trial on key accused of 1984 Delhi anti-sikh riots.
.
Please note that SoMoke , CoBhAp have opposed narcotest in public on key riot accused of 1984 delhi riots.
.
How do we generate whole campaign? I suggest following steps
.
1. Pls see the links to law-drafts and "order your MP via SMS" campaign , I have proposed in tinyurl .com/RahulMehtaSMS .
.
2. Please prepare a similar draft for narcotest in public by Jury over 1984 riot accused,
.
3. Request voters to send orders to MPs via SMS to print that draft in Gazette.
.
=====
.
SoMoKe = Sonia, NaMo, AK have opposed the proposal to narcotest 1984 riot accused in public, Proof : ask them. And their andhbhagats are opposing the narcotest in public , because if they support narcotest in public then it will weaken the image of their tall leaders.
.
We can and must use this fact to inform all activists and voters than SoMoKe-andbhagats are committed to their tall leaders, and not justice. And their tall leaders are scared of committing to justice.

Pawan Jury BhilwaraRj

Sat Nov 01 2014 19:53:03 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

आठवीं बोर्ड करना ही पर्याप्त नहीं है, सात्य प्रणाली तथा जिला शिक्षा अधिकारी को प्रजा अधीन किये बिना शैक्षिक सुधार दूर कि कौड़ी है।
_________________________
राजस्थान की मुख्यमंत्री वसुंधरा राजे ने 8 वीं को फिर से बोर्ड करने का फैसला किया है । ज्ञातव्य है कि 2009 में RTE का बहाना लेकर मनमोहन सरकार ने आठवीं बोर्ड को निरस्त कर दिया था ।
यह FDI EFFECT है कि हमारे सरकारी स्कूल बद से बदतरीन होते जा रहे है और सरकारे सुधार के नाम पर खानापूर्ति कर रही है ।
.
विज्ञान और गणित की शिक्षा से ही तकनीक आती है, तथा सरकारी स्कूलों का अच्छा स्तर धर्मांतरण के अवसरों को कमजोर कर देता है, इसीलिए बहुराष्ट्रीय कम्पनियाँ एवं मिशनरीज प्रलोभन/दबाव का इस्तेमाल करके ऐसे क़ानून पास करवाती है जिस से विज्ञान और गणित का आधारभूत ढांचा चरमरा जाए ।
.
सरकारी स्कूलों की बदहाली के चलते पिछले दशक भर में लगभग आधा मध्यवर्ग बलात रूप से निजी स्कूलों की शरण में धकेला जा चुका है, जबकि सरकारी स्कूल निम्न और मध्य वर्ग के भविष्य के साथ खिलवाड़ कर रहे है । निम्न वर्ग के पास शिक्षा के लिए सरकारी स्कूलों के अलावा कोई विकल्प नही है, क्योंकि निजी स्कूल उनकी पहुँच से बाहर है ।
वैसे मध्य वर्ग भी कम घाटे में नही रहा है । उनके पास विकल्प के तौर पर सिर्फ निजी स्कूल बचे है जो कि उनकी गर्दन मरोड़ कर नर्सरी और एल केजी के भी उतने पैसे वसूल रहे है, जितने में सरकारी स्कूलों में उच्च माध्यमिक तक की शिक्षा पूर्ण हो जाती है ।
.
कई शहरो में अभिभावक संघर्ष सिमितियाँ स्कुल संघो से फ़ीस इत्यादि कम करवाने के लिए अंतहीन संघर्ष में मुब्तिला है लेकिन सरकारी स्कूलो की दुर्दशा सुधारने को लेकर उदासीन बनी हुयी है, जो कि समस्या की जड़ है ।
करोड़ो अभिभावको को यह अहसास ही नहीं है कि, उनके खून पसीने की कमाई शिक्षा के नाम पर उनसे इसीलिए लूटी जा रही है, क्योंकि सभी सरकारे MNCs और मिशनरीज़ के दबाव में सरकारी स्कूलों को तबाह करने पर तुली हुयी है ।
.
तो आठवीं बोर्ड किये जाने के फैसले के बाद कितने अभिभावक अपने बच्चो को निजी स्कूलों से निकालकर सरकारी स्कूलों को भर्ती करवाने का विचार कर रहे है ?
जवाब है, शून्य ।
क्योंकि अभिभावक जानते है, कि कुछ भी बदलने वाला नही है ।

===

समाधान :

प्रजा अधीन राजा समूह ने शिक्षा सुधार के लिए क़ानून ड्राफ्ट्स प्रस्तावित किये है, जिन्हें गेजेट में छापने से हम सरकारी स्कूलों का स्तर निजी स्कूलों से बेहतर बना सकते है ।

1. प्रजा अधीन जिला शिक्षा अधिकारी : जिला स्तर पर नीतियों के निष्पादन एवं स्कूलों के कुशल प्रबंधन के लिए जिला शिक्षा अधिकारी जिम्मेदार होता है, अत: हम मांग कर रहे है कि जिला शिक्षा अधिकारी क्षेत्र के अभिभावकों द्वारा चुना जाए तथा क्षेत्र के अभिभावकों के पास उसे मतदान द्वारा नौकरी से निकालने की शक्ति हो ।
ये क़ानून पास होने से शिक्षा माफिया, नेता और अधिकारियों का गठजोड़ टूट जाएगा, और शिक्षा अधिकारी ईमानदारी से प्रजा के हित में कार्य करेगा ।
निकम्में और भ्रष्ट अधिकारी को अभिभावक मतदान द्वारा किसी भी समय नौकरी से निकाल सकेंगे, जिस से जिला शिक्षा अधिकारी जनता के प्रति जवाबदेह बना रहेगा ।
( ड्राफ्ट का लिंक नीचे दिया गया है)
.
2. प्रजा अधीन शिक्षा मंत्री :
शैक्षिक नीतियाँ विधान सभा एवं लोक सभा में बनायी जाती है, अत: हम मांग कर रहे है कि मानव संसाधन मंत्री तथा राज्यों के शिक्षा मंत्रियों को नौकरी से निकालने का अधिकार सीधे जनता के पास हो ।
.
3. गणित एवं विज्ञान की शिक्षा में गुणवत्ता बढाने के लिए गणित एवं विज्ञान में सात्य प्रणाली :
गणित एवं विज्ञान का आधार उच्च प्राथमिक एवं माध्यमिक कक्षाओं में तैयार होता है । इस आधार को मज़बूत करने के लिए हमें उन प्रतिभाशाली शिक्षको की आवश्यकता है, जिनमे शिक्षक तत्व* हो । गणित और विज्ञान में पिछड़ने के कारण हम तकनीक में पिछड़ गए है, तकनीक के अभाव में भारत स्वदेशी आधुनिक हथियारों का उत्पादन नहीं कर पा रहा है, और हमारी सेना विदेशी हथियारों पर निर्भर हो गयी है ।
सात्य प्रणाली आने से जहाँ एक और अभिभावकों द्वारा शिक्षा पर किये जाने वाले खर्चे में भारी कमी आएगी वहीँ दूसरी और शिक्षा की गुणवत्ता उत्कृष्ट स्तर की हो जायेगी ।
(सात्य प्रणाली की प्रक्रिया अंत में दी गयी है )
.
4. शिक्षको के स्थानान्तरण तथा निलंबन के लिए नागरिको की ज्यूरी प्रक्रिया :
शिक्षको को जनता के प्रति जवाबदेह बनाने के लिए हम मांग कर रहे है कि स्कूल स्टाफ के विवादों तथा स्थानान्तरण/निलंबन के फैसले क्षेत्र के अभिभावको की ज्यूरी द्वारा हो । इस क़ानून से निकम्मे और लापरवाह शिक्षको को ज्यूरी द्वारा हटाया जा सकेगा तथा सजग शिक्षको को पदोन्नति के निष्पक्ष अवसर मिलेंगे ।
.
5. सरकारी स्कूलों में अंगेजी माध्यम का प्रावधान : अधिकतर अभिभावक अपने बच्चो को निजी स्कूलों में पढ़ाने के लिए इसीलिए मजबूर है, क्योंकि सरकारी स्कूल अंग्रेजी के अध्यापन में पिछड़े हुए है ।
हम मांग कर रहे है कि सरकारी स्कूलों में पाठ्य पुस्तको को द्विभाषी बनाया जाए। इस प्रकार पुस्तक का एक पृष्ठ स्थानीय भाषा (सम पृष्ठ संख्या) में होगा, तथा अगले पृष्ठ (विषम पृष्ठ संख्या) पर उसका अंग्रेजी में अनुवाद होगा । छात्र को सुविधा होगी कि वह इच्छित माध्यम में परीक्षा दे सके ।
.
6. छात्रों को क़ानून की शिक्षा देना : देश को क़ानून चलाते है अत: प्रत्येक नागरिक को क़ानून व्यवस्था की बुनियादी समझ होना आवश्यक है, लेकिन हम छात्रों को कानूनों की जानकारी नही दे रहे है। हम मांग कर रहे है कि, क़ानून की किताबो का सरलीकरण किया जाए तथा कक्षा 5 से क़ानून की शिक्षा सभी छात्रों के लिए अनिवार्य की जाए ।

===

*शिक्षक तत्व- कुछ व्यक्तियों में व्याख्या करने की अच्छी क्षमता होती है तथा वे किसी भी बिन्दू या मसले को अपेक्षाकृत सुबोध और सुगम तरीके से समझा पाते है , तथा उन्हें ऐसा करने में संतोष का अनुभव होता है । विज्ञान और गणित जैसे जटिल विषयों में यदि हम ऐसे शिक्षको को आकर्षित कर पाए तो हमारे छात्रों की प्रतिभा को तराशा जा सकेगा । दूसरा लाभ प्रतिस्पर्धा का मिलेगा और शिक्षण विधियों में नए आयामों की खोज के अवसर मिलेंगे । सात्य प्रणाली का उपयोग अंग्रेजी तथा हिंदी ( साहित्य) में भी किया जा सकेगा ।
_______

गणित विज्ञान के लिए सात्य प्रणाली की प्रक्रिया :

1. जो भी गणित का शिक्षक बनना चाहता है वो DEO के पास 100 रूपये देकर अपना नाम पंजीकृत करवा सकेगा ।
2. शिक्षक स्वतंत्र रूप से छात्र जुटाएगा तथा उन्हें पढ़ायेगा ।
3. छात्र या अभिभावक इस के लिए शिक्षक को कोई भुगतान नही करेंगे ।
4. परीक्षाए DEO आयोजित करवाएगा तथा परिणामो के आधार पर ग्रेड देगा ।
5. परीक्षाए मासिक तथा वार्षिक होगी । ( मासिक- बहुविकल्पीय )
6. औसत से अधिक अंक लाने वाले छात्रों की वरीयता सूची बनेगी ।
7. वरीय ग्रेड के आधार पर शिक्षक को पुरूस्कार दिया जाएगा । जो पुरस्कार शिक्षक को मिलेगा वही छात्र को भी मिलेगा ।
8. शिक्षक को अन्य कोई वेतन भत्ते आदि देय नही होगा ।
9. यदि छात्र चाहे तो सत्र के मध्य में भी अपने शिक्षक के बदल सकेगा ।
10. पुरस्कार की राशि संपत्ति कर से वितरित की जायेगी ( संपत्ति कर का 2% )

उदाहरन के लिए क X एक अध्यापक 50 छात्रों को पढाता है जिसमे से 10 छात्र ग्रेड A तथा 5 छात्र ग्रेड A+ हासिल करते है, तो 5000 × 5 = 25000 , 1000 × 10 = 10000, । कुल 35000 रूपये शिक्षक प्राप्त करेगा ( उदाहरण वार्षिक आधार पर दिया गया है )
.
इन सभी कानूनों के ड्राफ्ट्स दिए गए लिंक पर देखे :
rahulmehta . com/301 . h. htm
(chapter - 30, 23)

नागरिको से निवेदन है कि अपने क्षेत्र के विधायक तथा सांसद को SMS द्वारा ऑर्डर भेजे, कि सात्य प्रणाली और ज्यूरी ट्रायल का ड्राफ्ट गेजेट में छापा जाए ।

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Mon Nov 03 2014 04:12:27 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Getting law-drafts to reduce 50 problems such as corruption or gohatya or bangladeshi or reduce inefficiencies of factory owners or fix taxation; and spreading information about these law-drafts .
.
For all activists, I say we require 50 versions of mini-301.pdf !!! And this booklet at fb . com/groups/righttorecallparty/10152747542153103/ (see link in comment) is the first such version !!!
.
Why do we need 50 version of mini-301.pdf?
.
Because true activists aka non-80G-activists happen to be in 50 different state of mind.
.
One set wants to solve goraksha problem FIRST and other problems later. Second set wants to solve corruption problem first and other problems later. The third set wants to solve factory owners problems first and all problems later. The fifth set wants to solve Bangladeshi problem first and everything else later. And so on.
.
With all flaws in them, these activists are better than rest 98% of population who dont want to spend any time on any cause.
.
So IMO, we need to write 50 booklets. IMO, we need to write one booklet for each set of activist. The booklet should explain how RTR over which key persons reduces his specific problems. How Jury System reduces his specific problems. How applicable economic law, be subsidy allocation or taxation, reduces his specific problem. All topics of 301.pdf will not apply -- some 10% will. Some 5% such as RTR, JutySys, TCP etc will apply on almost all problems
.
Each booklet should explain how RTR, JurySys, TCP, MRCM etc law-drafts solve his SPECIFIC problem and not talk about other problems.
.
And finally the booklet MUST have full text of SMS with URL links (and torrent links in future) , which activists can send as order to MP via SMS.
.
So each set of activists will read 1-2 booklets, see how HIS SPECIFIC problems reduce via RTR, TCP, JurySys, economic allocation laws , tax laws, And then some may read whole 301.pdf later. Basically, each booklet needs one or more complete modules. Each module is
.
a) law-draft text
.
b) url to fb-status where law-draft is posted . or better , magnet torrent link to law-draft
.
c) the text of sms to containing url or magnet torrent link
.
d) basic instructions to activists on giving newspaper avt of draft, sms etc.
.
The goraksha booklet explains this in detail. Here, many law-drafts are stated from gorakshak's PoV and makes no attempt to drag him to towards activism on anything else.
.
I request all recallist to read goraksha booklet from Right to recall Group files section, and write 50 more such booklet. One booklet for each problem.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Tue Nov 04 2014 02:55:59 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Issue of Foreign companies in Coal mining
.
Decision of SoMoKe = Sonia, NaMo, AK to allow foreign companies in coal mining business can create a huge hole in forex repatriation business. Coal mining is highly profitable. Now consider following scneraio
.
mining cost , including ALL costs = say Rs 1200 per tonne
.
royalty = say Rs 400 per tonne
.
sale price in market = Rs 2000 per tonne
.
final net profits before tax = Rs 400 per tonne
.
tax = 40% to 0% !! = Rs 120 to Rs 0 per tonne
.
profit after tax = Rs 280 to Rs 400 per tonne
.
Now a foreign company has option to pay 40% tax and demand repatriation of remaining Rs 280 in dollar !! IOW, a company digs coal inside India, sells coal inside India, and GoI is bound to pay dollars for its profits !! Further, is the company is from Mauritius or Singapore, then it can get away with even this 40% tax and demand repatriation of full Rs 400 of profits it made !! Mauritius/Singapore companies are exempt from tax on capital gains, bit operating profits. But there are ways to "convert" operating profits into short term capital gains and thus get away from taxes on whole of Rs 400 of profit. Now some say that profits in coal mining arent just 25% of final wholesale prices, but much much higher !!! In such case, repatriation burden can be much higher.
.
Now since GoI is always short of dollars, GoI often agrees to give tax exemption if profits are kept in India for say 5-10 years. So in such case, the repatriation crisis problem gets postponed for 5-10 years but hits with extra 40% saved taxes after that 5-10 years duration. And during these 5-10 years, the foreign company enjoys zero tax. So local company faces 35% corporate taxes, while foreign company will face zero tax rate . So after 3-4 years, the foreign company will have much higher capital and work with higher scale. With higher scale, by making contractual agreements with buyers to buy huge amounts from them, they can push out other sellers and eventually force them for takeover. So a big part of coal business may land up with foreign companies aka MNC-owners in my lingo.
.
That aside, there is big hole in information voters and even politically active activists have about repatriation issue. They voters and activists havent considered the fact that every dollar that comes into India via FDI can create burden of repatriation of one dollar plus profits plus capital gains minus taxes, and sometimes taxes can be zero if company is from Singapore or Mauritius. Apparently, SoMoKe and other leaders seem to have made no attempt of educating voters and activists about possibility of repatriation crisis and sovereign defaults , and its effects.
.
Whats makes things worse is that Sonia, AK both too have supported giving away coal mines to MNC-owners. AAP has 4 MPs in Loksabha. And none has proposed law-draft as private member's bill to oppose entry of foreigners into coal mining. And so CoBhApSwaRa-workers are all going door to door asking voters to support the decision of giving away coal mines to foreign companies.
.
Those who wish to lessen the mess should order their MPs via SMS with URL to WOIC draft, and proposed law to allow only WOIC = wholly owned companies by Indians (search for WOIC in rahulmehta .com/301.htm ),. I havent posted WOIC draft on FB and I dont have URL. So some activist can post such draft and obtain a URL and send that MP as order via SMS . And then ask more and more voters to send orders to MPs via SMS .
.
And those who wish to reduce the mess should also stop wasting time with CoBhApSwaRa-workers and SoMoKe-andhbhagats. And focus on "order your MO via SMS with URL with law-drafts needed for India" campaign. These CoBhApSwaRa-workers and SoMoKe-andhbhagats are shamelessly supporting entry of foreign companies in coal mining business and so IMO they are all good for thing except slogan shouting and adding noise pollution.

Read full post →

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Tue Nov 04 2014 03:42:13 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

How operating profits can be converted into short term capital gains?
.
The operating profits including interest earned of Mauritius\Singapore companies are taxable but their short term and long capital gains arent taxable, Now here is a way how a group of investors can convert its operating profit into short term capital gains.
.
Say company A is main company, which is Mauritius based
.
The owners starts company-B as independent company.
.
Company-B floats a bond of Rs 100 payable after 1 year. Since company-B has no track record no buyer will show up. So company A buys the bond at low price, say Rs 50 !!
.
Company-B gets huge contract in railways or coal mining etc etc. So its bond's prices increase to as high as Rs 85 to Rs 89.
.
Company-A sells bonds to buyers at Rs 85 to Rs 89. Company-A made short term capital gains, which is tax free since it is based in Mauritius.
.
Say company-B earned Rs 50 of operating profit from mining or that contract. Now it pays Rs 100 for redemption of bond after 1 year. So tax on company-B will be zero.
.
The second buyer will pay normal tax on interest earned from the bond.
.
This is one way. They are several ways.
,
So the clause that only capital gains are exempt, but operating profits will be taxed isnt much useful.
.
Solution is to tax Mauritius\Singapore companies at par with Indian companies. But SoMoKe, SoMoKe-supporters and CoBhApSwaRa-workers have opposed this proposal. They all insist on continuation of Mauritius\Singapore route. The 4 AAP MPs and all Congress\BJP have refused to submit private member's bill to end Mauritius\Singapore route.
.
So activist who want to end Mauritius route should boycott slogan shouting, cap wearing, mask wearing, broom welding, jhadu welding CoBhAp workers and focus on "order your MP via SMS containing URL to law-draft". Some activist should post proposed law-draft to cancel Mauritius \ Singapore route. And pls stop wasting time with slogan shouters and their broom weilding and jhadu weilding.

Read full post →

Pawan Jury BhilwaraRj

Wed Nov 05 2014 06:38:52 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

मिशनरीज़ तथा MNCs द्वारा संचालित पेड मिडिया को खुश करने के लिए मोदी साहब ने परेश रावल को हिन्दू धर्म की खिल्ली उड़ाने के निर्देश दिए है ।
.
इंडियन एक्सप्रेस में छपी खबर के मुताबिक़ परेश रावल ने कहा है कि
'पूजा स्थलों में हिन्दू मंदिर सबसे ज्यादा गंदे है' ।
ज्ञातव्य है कि परेश रावल ने MNCs द्वारा वित्त पोषित 'ओह माय गॉड' नामक फीचर फिल्म में हिन्दू देवालयों तथा मूर्ती पूजा का जमकर मज़ाक उड़ाया था, जिससे उनके लोकसभा चुनाव के टिकेट का मार्ग प्रशस्त हुआ ।
मोदी साहब खुद 'देवालय से पहले शौचालय' तथा हर्षवर्धन 'आतिशबाजी मुक्त दिवाली' के ऐलान दे चुके है, जबकि उमा भारती को लगता है कि गंगा को औद्योगिक अपशिष्ट और मलमूत्र वाही नालो से ज्यादा मृतको की हड्डियाँ और राख प्रदूषित कर रही है ।
.

गौरतलब है कि ये सभी हिंदूवादी नेता पेड मिडिया पर पूरी तरह से निर्भर है , इसलिए मिशनरीज़ को खुश रखने के लिए सार्वजनिक तौर पे हिन्दू धर्म का उपहास करते है, और उन कानूनों का विरोध करते है, जिनसे मंदिरों के प्रशासन को सुधारा जा सके । यदि हिन्दू मंदिर 'गंदे' है, तो क्या मोदी साहब उम्मीद कर रहे है कि मुलायम सिंह यादव और अससुद्दीन ओवैसी मंदिरों को सुधारने के लिए क़ानून बनायेंगे !!!
या सोनिया गांधी को इसकी परवाह करनी चाहिए ?
_______

समाधान :
प्रजा अधीन राजा समूह ने हिन्दू देवालयों के नियमन तथा प्रबंधन के लिए क़ानूनी ड्राफ्ट प्रस्तावित किया है। नागरिको से निवेदन है कि अपने क्षेत्र के सांसद को SMS द्वारा ऑर्डर भेजे कि, इस क़ानून को गेजेट में छापा जाए ।
rahulmehta . com/301. h. htm
(chapter - 35)

.

.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Wed Nov 05 2014 18:34:09 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Day before yesterday, I had to sit in police station for 4 hours ; there was a slim possibility of overnght stay. But nothing happened. IT WAS NOT POLITICAL HARASSMENT AT ALL!! no political head was behind it.
.
Basically, there was a minor fault of mine. I had rented flat to some Bipinbhai, and I had given copy of the contract to police station as needed. But contract had expired , same tenant stayed, same rent stayed and I didnt make and register new contract.
.
Now Bipinbhai was running genuine Paying Guest = PG business. And one of his PG lost mobile phone. So he went to file FIR so that he can claim insurance etc. So constable asked him for address, name of owner, name of main tenant etc and asked him to get copy of contract, and the proof that the contract was registered with police. I went with contract, the form etc, and it had expired over six months back. So technically, I was at fault. I went there at about 4pm day before yesterday i.e. monday.
.
Now, normal procedure is to ask owner to finish process in 3 days. But instead, I was told that there may be police case, and I will need bail or else I will be locked up under section abc and section def etc etc. I asked if "self bail" will be applicable. Generally, in all such minor violations, when person has no other case against him, self bail is valid. But person there said no to self bail. In fact, one person there even said that "this crime isnt bailable" !!! So I said "fine, I will ask friend of mine to come for bail". My friend said that he would come to the police station at 9pm. And I told them that my freind would come at 9pm with bail papers. Worst case --- I would have been in lock up for 1 day and then released next day. I was ready for that.
.
Finally, at 8pm, they asked me to go, and asked me to get all papers registered in 3 days !!
.
It was NOT a politically motivated case at all. But still, experience was worrying. What if I had not known internal guidelines. What if I had believed his word that "my offence wasnt bailable" !!
.
Long term solution is ---- Jury System, and that law-education should start in 8th class or whenever parents say. If parents say that "start law education in class-1" then it should start in class-1. This is proposal all SoMoKe-supporters, SoMoKe, CoBhAp have opposed.
.
Short term solution is : always keep property tax bill and its xerox copies ready with you all the time !! To give bail to your friend, you must have a property in your name, and you must have property tax bill with YOUR name on it and you need to have true xerox copy of it. And the property owner must come to bail the accuse. Many times, self bail is accepted. In such case too, xerox of property tax bill may be needed.
.

Those who want to know the truth will start searching for truth and not ask for "proof". So dont waste time with people who say "do you have proofs?".
.
Many times, I write posts giving SoMoKe's actions on many laws that are being passed in India or not passed in India. eg I once said that "SoMoKe oppose Citizens' review over judiciary as practiced in USA, Japana and Jury System as practiced in USA,". And each time , I make such sentences, their andhbhagats run and ask me "give me a proof". Well, I just request them not to waste their time on my wall. I and I request all recallists , to ask all this proof-seekers not to waste time to walls on recallists. Trust me, these proof-seekers are useless people and will never be useful in our goal of getting law-drafts like MRCM, JurySys, RTR, TCP etc.
.
Because those who want to know truth would never ask useless questions like "give me proof that SoMoKe oppose Citizens' review over judiciary as practiced in USA, Japana and Jury System as practiced in USA,"
.
Why?
.
Because if he wanted to know truth, he would take following steps
.
1. He would have GOOGLED on what is citizen's review over judiciary, japan, USA etc keywords and found it
.
2. He would read the constitutional amendment NaMo printed wrt judiciary some 2 months ago.
.
3. He would have obtained private member's bills congress, app MPs proposed wrt judiciary.
.
4. He would have tried to see if that constitutional amendment or those private member's bills have citizens' review over Judiciary or Jury System.
.
5. And then decided on his own whether statement that "SoMoKe oppose Citizens' review over judiciary as practiced in USA, Japan and Jury System as practiced in USA," is true or false all by himself.
.
But instead of spending time in googling and reading the constitutional amendments and private members' bills, they jump and say "give me the proof that ....". Well, I ask them to further save their time by staying away from my wall !!
.
AFAIS , I didnt lose anything by losing them. My goal is to take some pieces of INFORMATION to all 82 crore adult voters and other 20 crore non-voters who are abiove the age of 10 years. What pieces of information?
.
(1) that "there are countries like USA on our planet which have law-drafts like JurySys , RTR , wealth tax, referendum etc and they are ahead of us due to these law-drafts".
.
(2) least expensive way and least time consuming way to get these law-drafts printed in Indian Gazette is to make over 42 crore voters order MPs via SMS with URL or better URN to these law-drafts
.
The proof seekers arent going to be useful in step-1 and step-2 anyway. So IMO, I didnt lose anything by losing them.
.
So next time if anyone asks for proofs, pls ask him to decide on his own, and then not waste time on your walls.

Trust me, these proof-seekers are useless people and will never be useful in our goal of getting law-drafts like MRCM, JurySys, RTR, TCP etc.
.
Because those who want to know truth would never ask useless questions like "give me proof that SoMoKe oppose Citizens' review over judiciary as practiced in USA, Japana and Jury System as practiced in USA,"
.
Why?
.
Because if he wanted to know truth, he would take following steps
.
1. He would have GOOGLED on what is citizen's review over judiciary, japan, USA etc keywords and found it
.
2. He would read the constitutional amendment NaMo printed wrt judiciary some 2 months ago.
.
3. He would have obtained private member's bills congress, app MPs proposed wrt judiciary.
.
4. He would have tried to see if that constitutional amendment or those private member's bills have citizens' review over Judiciary or Jury System.
.
5. And then decided on his own whether statement that "SoMoKe oppose Citizens' review over judiciary as practiced in USA, Japan and Jury System as practiced in USA," is true or false all by himself.
.
But instead of spending time in googling and reading the constitutional amendments and private members' bills, they jump and say "give me the proof that ....". Well, I ask them to further save their time by staying away from my wall !!
.
AFAIS , I didnt lose anything by losing them. My goal is to take some pieces of INFORMATION to all 82 crore adult voters and other 20 crore non-voters who are abiove the age of 10 years. What pieces of information?
.
(1) that "there are countries like USA on our planet which have law-drafts like JurySys , RTR , wealth tax, referendum etc and they are ahead of us due to these law-drafts".
.
(2) least expensive way and least time consuming way to get these law-drafts printed in Indian Gazette is to make over 42 crore voters order MPs via SMS with URL or better URN to these law-drafts
.
The proof seekers arent going to be useful in step-1 and step-2 anyway. So IMO, I didnt lose anything by losing them.
.
So next time if anyone asks for proofs, pls ask him to decide on his own, and then not waste time on your walls.

Pawan Jury BhilwaraRj

Thu Nov 06 2014 08:10:36 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

दिल्ली के लिए बीजेपी ने मुख्यमंत्री के उम्मीदवार की घोषणा नही करने का फैसला किया है ।
क्यों ???
.
1. मोदी और शाह पार्टी पर पकड़ बनाने और अपने कद को और ऊँचा उठाने के लिए यह जोखिम उठा रहे है ।
A. क्योंकि यदि चुनाव पूर्व मुख्यमंत्री के उम्मीदवार की घोषणा कर दी जाए, तो जनादेश उम्मीदवार के खाते में चला जाता है, जिससे आलाकमान (मोदी, शाह) 5 वर्षो के दौरान मुख्यमंत्री को बदल सकने का नैतिक अधिकार खो देते है ।
B. क्योंकि इससे क्षेत्रीय नेताओ का कद बढ़ता है जो कि कालान्तर में आलाकमान के लिए चुनौती बन सकता है ।
C. क्योंकि जीत का पूरा श्रेय आलाकमान को देने में बाधा आती है ।
उदाहरण - शिवराज, रमन सिंह, वसुंधरा, मनोहर पर्रिकर ।( ताकतवर क्षत्रप)
फड़नवीस, खट्टर । ( कठपुतली क्षत्रप )
.
2. बीजेपी ने MNCs और मिशनरीज़ से केजरीवाल को मुख्यमंत्री बनाने के लिए सेफ कोरिडोर देने का समझौता किया है ।
A. हरियाणा में केजरीवाल ने अपने उम्मीदवार नही उतार कर बीजेपी को फायदा पहुंचाया था ।
B. हालाकिं मोदी MNCs के एजेंडे पर ही कार्य कर रहे है, किन्तु MNCs यह संतुलन साधने की कोशिश कर रही है कि मोदी को काबू में बनाए रखने के लिए केजरीवाल लगातार प्रासंगिक बने रहे ।
.
3. बीजेपी रणनिति के तहत ऐसा कर रही है, अत: चुनावपूर्व मुख्यमंत्री का उम्मीदवार घोषित कर देगी ।
___________

¶ हालांकि फैसला जनता करेगी, किन्तु इसमें कोई दो राय नहीं कि यदि बीजेपी मुख्यमंत्री का उम्मीदवार घोषित नही करती है, तो ज़्यादातर संभावना है कि बीजेपी का प्रचार, माकूल चेहरा नही होने से कमज़ोर पड़ जाएगा, और केजरीवाल बढ़त बना लेंगे ।
___________________

Pawan Jury BhilwaraRj

Fri Nov 07 2014 07:49:12 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

हथियारबंद नागरिक समाज अधिनियम 2014
____________________

1. जिलाधीश के लिए निर्देश :
यदि कोई नागरिक मतदाता इस क़ानून में संशोधन चाहता है, तो वह वांछित संशोधन के लिए एक शपथपत्र जिलाधीश कार्यालय में दे सकेगा । इस शपथपत्र को जिलाधीश या उसका सहायक प्रति पृष्ठ 20 रू की दर से शुल्क लेकर प्राधानमंत्री की वेबसाईट पर डाल देगा ।
2. पटवारी/तलाटी के लिए निर्देश :
यदि कोई नागरिक मतदाता ऐसे शपथपत्र पर या उसमें वर्णित किसी धारा में बदलाव के लिए हां/ना दर्ज कराता है, तो पटवारी ऐसी राय को 3 रू लेकर दर्ज करेगा तथा इसे प्रधानमन्त्री की वेबसाईट पर डाल देगा ।
3. हाँ या ना की संख्या के आधार पर प्रधानमन्त्री/मुख्यमंत्री फैसला लेने के लिए बाध्य नही होंगे ।
4. प्रधानमंत्री के लिए निर्देश :
प्रधानमंत्री हर जिले में एक जिला आयुध अधिकारी की नियुक्ति करेंगे।
प्रधानमन्त्री जिला आयुध अधिकारी को प्रजा अधीन करने की प्रक्रिया को गेजेट में प्रकाशित करेंगे ।
5. रक्षा मंत्री के लिए निर्देश :
रक्षा मंत्री निम्न लिखित श्रेणी के हथियारों की सूचियाँ प्रकाशित करेगा -
(A) छोटी बंदूके।
(B) मध्यम आकार की बंदूके।
(C) बड़ी बंदूके।
(D) होवित्ज़र तथा होवित्ज़र किस्म के अन्य प्रकार।
(E) टेंक तथा टेंक के अन्य प्रकार।
(F) कारतूस, गोले तथा कारतूस व गोलों के अन्य प्रकार।
(G) प्रक्षेपास्त्र तथा प्रक्षेपास्त्र के अन्य प्रकार।
(H) लड़ाकू विमान तथा लड़ाकू विमानो के अन्य प्रकार।
(I) सेना द्वारा उपयोग किये जाने वाले अन्य उपकरण ।
6. सभी के लिए निर्देश :
कोई भी नागरिक जिसकी उम्र 22 वर्ष से अधिक तथा कुल संपत्ति 10 लाख से अधिक हो (इस संपत्ति में एक घर जिसकी कीमत 1 करोड़ तथा फर्नीचर जिसकी कीमत 10 लाख हो, को शामिल नही किया जाएगा ), के लिए एक छोटी बन्दूक तथा 100 कारतूस अपने घर पर रखना आवश्यक होगा ।
कोई भी नागरिक जिसकी उम्र 22 वर्ष से अधिक तथा कुल संपत्ति 20 लाख से अधिक हो (इस संपत्ति में एक घर जिसकी कीमत 1 करोड़ तथा फर्नीचर जिसकी कीमत 10 लाख हो, को शामिल नही किया जाएगा ), के लिए एक मध्यम आकार की बन्दूक तथा 100 कारतूस अपने घर पर रखना आवश्यक होगा ।
कोई भी नागरिक जिसकी उम्र 22 वर्ष से अधिक तथा कुल संपत्ति 30 लाख से अधिक हो (इस संपत्ति में एक घर जिसकी कीमत 1 करोड़ तथा फर्नीचर जिसकी कीमत 10 लाख हो, को शामिल नही किया जाएगा ), के लिए एक बड़ी बन्दूक तथा 100 कारतूस अपने घर पर रखना आवश्यक होगा ।
कोई भी नागरिक किसी भी श्रेणी की दो बंदूके रख सकेगा, दो से अधिक बंदूके रखने के लिए ऐसे व्यक्ति को जिला पुलिस कमिश्नर से लाइसेंस लेना होगा ।
7. कोई भी नागरिक जिसकी उम्र 22 वर्ष से अधिक तथा संपत्ति 10 लाख से कम होगी, छोटी, मध्यम या बडे आकार की कोई भी बन्दूक अपने घर पर रख सकेगा ।
8. कोई भी नागरिक जिस भी श्रेणी की बंदूक रखेगा, उसके लिए उस बन्दूक को जिला आयुध अधिकारी तथा जिला पुलिस कमिश्नर कार्यालय में रजिस्टर कराना आवश्यक होगा ।
9. जिला आयुध अधिकारी, जिला पुलिस कमिश्नर, कोई भी नागरिक और ग्रांड ज्युरर्स के लिए निर्देश :
जिला आयुध अधिकारी, जिला पुलिस कमिश्नर या कोई भी नागरिक, किसी भी व्यक्ति के हथियार रखने को प्रतिबंधित करने के लिए जिला ग्रांड ज्यूरी से ज्यूरी ट्रायल के लिए निवेदन कर सकेगा ।
ग्रांड ज्यूरी यह बहुमत से यह निश्चय करेगी
कि क्या ज्यूरी द्वारा सुनवाई की जानी चाहिए या नही, ग्रांड ज्यूरी ज्युरर्स की संख्या भी तय करेगा । जिला ज्यूरी प्रशासन को को अधिकार होगा कि वह ज्यूरी के आकार को बढ़ा सके ।
10. ज्युरर्स के लिए निर्देश :
ज्युरर्स बहुमत से यह फैसला करेंगे कि, आरोपी के हथियार रखने को प्रतिबंधित किया जाए या नही, ज्युरर्स ऐसे प्रतिबन्ध की अवधी भी निर्धारित करेंगे । तय अवधि बीत जाने पर अन्य ज्यूरी यह फैसला करेगी कि, आरोपी को हथियार रखने की अनुमति दी जानी चाहिए या नही ।
11. जिला आयुध अधिकारी तथा नागरिको के लिए निर्देश :
यदि कोई नागरिक या जिला आयुध अधिकारी ग्रांड ज्युरर्स को शिकायत करता है कि, अमुक व्यक्ति निर्धारित नियमो के अनुसार बंदूक या कारतूस अपने घर पर नहीं रख रहा है, तो ग्रांड ज्युरर्स यह फैसला कर सकेंगे कि ऐसे आरोपी पर ज्यूरी द्वारा सुनवाई की जानी चाहिए या नहीं ।
12. ज्युरर्स के लिए निर्देश :
यदि यह आरोप सिद्ध होता है कि आरोपी निर्धारित सीमा के अनुसार बंदूक तथा कारतूस अपने घर पर नहीं रख रहा है, तो ज्युरर्स ऐसे व्यक्ति पर उसकी संपत्ति के 5% के अनुपात तक जुर्माना (इस संपत्ति में 1 करोड़ का घर तथा 10 लाख तक का फर्नीचर शामिल नही किया जाएगा ) और, या 2 माह के कारावास की सजा सुना सकेंगे ।
अपवादित मामलो को छोड़कर, प्रथम अपराध के लिए यह जुर्माना 2% तक, द्वितीय अपराध के लिए 4% तक तथा तीसरे तथा अन्य अपराधो के लिए यह जुर्माना 5% तक हो सकेगा । प्रथम अपराध कारावास द्वारा दंडनीय नहीं हो सकेगा ।
13. जिला आयुध अधिकारी के लिए निर्देश :
जिला आयुध अधिकारी प्रतिदिन ग्रांड ज्यूरी की उपस्थिति में मतदाता सूची से 0.01% वयस्क नागरिको का चयन अक्रमत: विधि से करेगा, तथा क्रमहीन विधि से औचक निरिक्षण करेगा कि अमुक नागरिक अपने घर पर निर्धारित सीमा के अनुसार बन्दूक तथा कारतूस रख रहे है या नही ।
14. जिला आयुध अधिकारी के लिए निर्देश :
नागरिको को बन्दूक, होवित्ज़र, टेंक, विमानरोधी प्रक्षेपास्त्र, हेलिकोप्टर तथा लड़ाकू विमान आदि चलाने का प्रशिक्षण देने के लिए, जिला आयुध अधिकारी उपकरणों, हथियारों और प्रशिक्षण मैदान की व्यवस्था करेगा, तथा इन गतिविधियों के संचालन के लिए आवश्यक निधि रक्षा मंत्री से प्राप्त करेगा। प्रशिक्षण शुल्क प्रशिक्षुओ द्वारा देय होगा, तथा दरें जिला आयुध अधिकारी द्वारा निर्धारित की जायेगी ।
15. जिला आयुध अधिकारी के लिए निर्देश :
प्रशिक्षण के लिए हथियारों तथा अन्य उपकरणों की खरीद आदि की व्यवस्थाओ के लिए जिला आयुध अधिकारी नागरिको से अनुदान तथा चंदे स्वीकार कर सकेगा ।
16. जिला आयुध अधिकारी के लिए निर्देश :
जिला आयुध अधिकारी नागरिको की श्रेणियों के अनुसार अनिवार्य तथा वैकल्पिक प्रशिक्षण के नियम निर्धारित करेगा। जिला आयुध अधिकारी के अनिवार्य प्रशिक्षण कार्यक्रम की घोषणा करने पर, जिला क्षेत्र में रहने वाले 22 से 50 वर्ष की वय के सभी नागरिको के लिए 2 वर्ष के भीतर प्रशिक्षण लेना अनिवार्य होगा ।
__________________

Mon Nov 10 2014 03:39:12 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

A post to explain religious conversion in India, and how CoBhApSwaRa-workers are indirectly helping conversions :
.
A moneylender A loans Rs 20000 to B. The conditions are --
.
1. B pays Rs 5000 interest on day one itself. So B gets Rs 15000
.
2. B has to pay Rs 100 for next 200 days
.
What is annual rate of interest per year?
.
PLEASE NOTE THAT PRINCIPAL CHANGES EVERYDAY !!
.
Please note that there is no such thing as flat interest. The interest is always "cut interest" i.e. "kapat vyaj". Also, interest in business world is never simple interest --- it is always compound interest. Simple interest is only in textbooks and sometimes while collecting fines. Also, interest is compounded daily or monthly or quarterly. So please state scenario you are considering.
.
Hint : use excel
.
And AFTER you have calculated interest rates, please explain how this post relates to conversions and how CoBhApSwaRa-workers are indirectly helping conversions.Please dont post comments BEFORE calculating interest rates.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Mon Nov 10 2014 09:44:38 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Answer to a common useless pro-FDI argument given by SoMoKe-andhbhagats and other FDI-supporters is --- "Why do you use FB and WhatsApp? They are also MNC-owners".
.
Answer is : Using FB / WA doesnt create repatriation burden. Using windows \ samsung creates a FIXED forex loss. Whereas FDI can cause potentially unlimited forex loss as GoI has to give dollars for all the profits that that foreign company earns.
.
The FDI is "FDI as implemented by GoI and opposition" i.e. SoMoKe. The existing rules of FDI as implemented by SoMoKe are
.
1. FDI companies can ask for dollars for their profits after paying 40% income tax.
.
2. And, sometimes, even than 40% tax is waived !!! eg Mauritius companies, Singapore companies etc dont pay any taxes on short/long capital gains and there are ways to convert operating profits into capital gains.
.
3. And if Foreign company decides to keep profits inside India, then most often, they are given full tax exemption, which kills local businesses.
.
None of these argument apply when one uses FD, WhatsApp etc as they are free.
.
If you use western Windows or Mobile, then also, forex loss is fixed. It does NOT create any FUTURE liabilities of forex. Whereas when a foreign company is allowed inside India, it creates potentially unbounded forex liability for its future profits.
.
The Chinese in the beginning had the rule that foreign company can only repatriate profits equal to or less than its export earnings. IOW, the FDI as implemented by Chinese in China in 1980-2000 was so that repatriation burden was zero.
.
Telling this to most SoMoKen-andbhagats would be like reading bhaagawat to a buffalo. All they like to do is slogan shouting, throw punchlines, make jinx, cite poems and come up with create fullforms like "FDI means First Develop India" followed by 4-digit-ID logic that "if you are anti-FDI then it means you are anti-development and anti-India !!" In fact, the most recent argument I got from NaMo-bhagats was " instead of bashing GoI policies, you should take broom and clean near by roads, gardens and slums !!". Saare tark par jhadu fer diya !!!
.
But still, it is our duty to spread the word, and so we should tell the importance of repatriation burden that debt, FDI etc create..

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Mon Nov 10 2014 13:02:18 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Dear recallists,
.
Thos who are following my status posts etc on wealh tax, taxation issues etc.
.
I have made following changes in the wealth tax proposal.
.
I have added a concept called as WTC = Wealth tax credit.
.
The start value will be taxes a person \ company has paid in past 4 years plus 15% of salaries he has paid in past 4 years. This will include income tax, net service tax, excise, sales tax and vat.
.
The wealth tax payable next year will be = 3% of market value of wealth above 250 sqft of land and 500 sqft of construction per person, minus 30% of actual salaries paid minus income tax payable - minus WTC.
.
If the number is negative , then it will become WTC for next year.
.
So say a person has office worth Rs 10 crore, And in past 4 years, income tax etc he has paid is Rs 30 lakhs. Say this year, his income is zero, The wealth tax credit = Rs 30 lakhs. And wealth tax payable this year will be zero.
.
For rented property, if the person has been staying there for over 12 months, then for 1 year, the wealth tax will be 30% of rent and later it will be 3% of market value of property
.
WTC will ensure that if a person has been paying income tax for long, then for rest of his retired life wealth tax on him will be near zero. WTC will not be inherited. And with 3% wealth tax, there is no need for inheritance tax.
.
But if person has been hoarding land, and generating no income, then wealth tax will force him to sell or rent his rent.
.
This will bring down land prices and will reduce COST OF DEVELOPMENT, and enable India to develop without FDI.
.
Here , I must add that SoMoKe oppose wealth tax, and insist on clumsy and regressive taxes like VAT, GST, Excise, CST etc. SoMoKe-andhbhagats do see some merit in wealth tax over GST. But if they support wealth tax, then SoMoKe will look bad. So they also oppose wealth tax and support GST --- India can go hell !!
.
Anyone who is pro-development will support wealth tax and oppose VAT, excise and GST. The fact that SoMoKe oppose wealth tax and support VAT, GST and excise proves that they are anti-development, and all their development talk is just paidmedia.
.
Solution I propose to activists is to understand how VAT's "buyer will pay for missing trader's default" clause will ruin small business and promote MNC-owners over Indian businesses. And how wealth tax reduces land prices. And inform more and more voters about these two laws. And order MPs via SMS to remove VAT, GST , Excise etc. This will strengthen the movement to repeal GST, VAT, Excise etc and bring wealth tax, even against the wish of SoMoKe. As movement progress, SoMoKe will use campaigns like Swachchh Bharat and Janlokpal etc to drain energy of activists, and if they fail, they will run to support wealth tax and claim credit !! But all that is distant future. What we can do now,is promote the concept of wealth tax.

Read full post →

b. dont talk about NaMo's hostility to law-drafts like wealth tax, that can bring down cost of non-FDI-led-development , but pick up broom and clean street.
.
c. dont talk about NaMo's decision to continue cancer cusing chemical pesticide, instead take broom and clean roads !!!
.
IOW, talk of anything and NaMo-andhbhagats will show a broom !!!
.

.

.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Fri Nov 14 2014 16:20:01 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Difference between sending order to MP via SMS (1) with URL \ URN and (2) without URL \ URI--- is same as difference between 145 years and 50 days !! And difference between Difference between sending order to MP via SMS (1) with URL and (2) with URN is same as difference between real Rs 1000 notes and potentially fake Rs 1000 note !!!
.
Dear all,
.
Pls dont ruin "order your MP via SMS" campaign by sending SMS without URL. It is almost as bad as slogan shouting. Let me explain how.
.
Each MP has about 17 lakh voters with him. The whole campaign is failure if only 1 lakh voters send no matter how many SMS. In such case, without or without SMS are both dud. But say over 8 lakh to 10 lakh voters do start sending orders to MPs via SMS. Now if each voter is sending only 1-2 orders on an average, then also campaign is dud. Lets say on an average each voter is sending 20 orders to MP via SMS, without URL.
.
Then MP gets some 3.5 crore SMS.
.
Say reading each SMS needs at least 30 seconds.
.
So MP or his staff would need = 3.5 crore SMS * 30 seconds per SMS = 105 crore seconds = 105/60 crore minutes = 1.75 crore minutes = 1.75/60 = 8.75 lakh hours
.
Say a man works 8 hours a day . So MP would need = 8.75/8 = 1.09 lakh days.
.
Say MP and his men work all 365 days a year. So he would need = 109,00 days / 330 = about 290 man years to process all these SMS.
.
Say some 50% SMS were dealing with some 100 law-drafts. Then also, MP would need same 290 man years, as he needs to go thru all SMS. Or he will need at least 145 years.
.
Now instead, if each voter sends URL, then MP's PC can aggregate them by url and give report
.
URL1 = 12.0 lakh SMS
URL2 = 11.6 lakh SMS
....
URL10 = 2.5 lakh SMS
...
....
URLN = below 1 lakh SMS
.....
.
So here too, if MP wishes to processes all 3.5 crore SMS, he would still need 145 years. But to process the SMS dealing with law-drafts supported by 1 lakh or more voters , the MPs need to take print out of draft pointed by these 50-100-200 URLs and read them. Each URL can have one draft , which could be 2-5 pages. So MP has to read some 300-500 pages, which he can do in 30-40 days at most.
.
IOW, if SMS have URL, then MP's PC can aggregate them and give counts. And so time taken to process law-drafts demanded by majority or over 5% of voters, reduces from 245 years to 30-40 days !! The ratio of 50 days to 250 years is 2240 to 1. IOW, SMS without URL is some 2240 times more time consuming than SMS with URL
.
So now many will still say that "SMS without URL is good", a logic which says "Rs 1 is also good and Rs 2000 is also good". This is sheer nonsense. SMS without URL have zero value, as no MP can process crores of SMS and decide what majority wants.
.
So please DO NOT ruin the proposal by sending SMS without URL.
.
Why oppose shortcodes?
===================
.
Shortcodes have values if MP is registering shortcodes after taking some fee. This is doable. And if MP does so, then SMS with shortcodes are better than SMS with URL. But so far, so MP does so. So shortcodes as of now, are useless.
.
Why are URN better than URL
=======================
.
As of now, URL are good enough. But as order your MP via SMS campaign picks up, URL will backfire as draft cited at URL can be tempered by owner of website such as facebook, or can be deleted by hacker or draft-writer. And so we will need to URNs. URNs are foolproof enough that even CIA cant temper it !! Please google on URN and URL . magnet links are easiest way to implement URNs. Within a few months, I will move to URNs and stop using URLs.
.
So all in all, please dont ruin the campaign by sending "orders to MPs via SMS" with no URL. Please make sure that YOUR SMS does have URL to the law-draft you want.
.

Read full post →

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Fri Nov 14 2014 16:29:12 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

NaMo orders 100% FDI in railway in 17 major areas. So GoI will have to pay dollars to foreign companies which make profits in these areas !!! IOW, chances of repatriation crisis drastically increases. see trak .in/tags/business/2014/11/13/indian-railways-100-fdi/
.
This should be seen with the FACT that NaMo has been opposing law-drafts needed to improve local companies, such as JurySys, RTR, wealth tax, TCP, removal of excise\GST\VAT\CST ,
.
===================
see trak .in/tags/business/2014/11/13/indian-railways-100-fdi/
.
Indian Railways Gets 100% FDI In 17 Key Areas
.
Indian Railways, which is facing a severe cash crunch to the tune of Rs 30,000 crore every year, received a major financial boost as 100% Foreign Direct Investment (FDI) is now allowed for developing infrastructure and improving safety features. The Railway Board and a Cabinet Panel has identified 17 key areas where this FDI can be used.
.
Ever since its origin, Indian Railways had always been shut off from receiving any kind of FDI, considering security risks involved. But in August this year, cabinet cleared FDI provision in the Indian Railways, and yesterday, the important areas were finally identified and notified to the general public.
.
It is expected that these new guidelines can attract upto Rs 90,000 crore FDI into Indian Railways.
.
Among others suggestions, proposal to build Bio-toilets in passenger coaches and mechanized / automated laundry system at every station are the ones which are getting huge attention, as the plans were in the pipeline since ages, but due to lack to funds the plans were never implemented.
.
Installation and maintenance of Bio-toilets in passenger coaches
.
Technological solutions for manned and unmanned level crossings
(Construction and maintenance of ROB/RUB /Limited Height Subway)
.
Technological solutions to improve safety and reduce accidents (Installation and maintenance of Asset failure detection systems (Track/ OHE/ Rolling Stock/Signaling etc.)
.
High Speed Train Projects: Projects involving those trains which will run above 250 Km/hr speed; and will have no connection or link with any existing railway line or route. The designer will have complete freedom to exercise his creativity and ideas; Government will also chip in with resources and money.)
.
Mechanized laundry (land will be leased by Ministry of Railways at Re 1 / annum)
.
Producing non-conventional energy from sources such as solar, tidal, wind etc with open market tender being offered.
.
Rolling stock procurement
.
Concessioning of standalone passenger corridors (branch lines, hill railways etc). (renovation of these lines, optimizing them for better commercial usage)
.
Testing facilities and world class laboratories for experimenting new technology
.
Setting up Railway Technical Training Institutes
.
Construction of world class passenger terminals and
renovation/maintenance of existing stations
.
Creation of Freight terminals/ Logistics Parks in strategically important locations
.
Signaling system – Construction of new facilities to develop advanced systems and renovating/maintaining existing systems
.
Railway Electrification
.
Rolling stock including train sets and locomotives or coach manufacturing and maintenance facilities.
.
Dedicated freight lines on a Joint Venture and/or PPP model, with clear revenue sharing guidelines (Private Trains on certain lines will also be allowed from now on..)
.
Suburban corridor projects through PPP: All new suburban corridor projects are permissible when launched through PPP route by MoR. The developer can construct, maintain and operate the corridor within the concession period.
.
The Railway Board has outlined a series of ‘terms and conditions’ which apply in this unique 100% FDI route, such as in those projects which are situated near border areas, FDI beyond 49% will be brought before the cabinet panel for due approval; safety clearance and audit are required for projects involving public carriage of passenger and more.
.

Read full post →

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sun Nov 16 2014 09:07:48 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

How SoMoKe's FDI proposals give a higher level to foreign companies and put Indian companies at a lower level.
.
SoMoKe = Sonia, Modi, AK , as far as FDI goes, all three are one and the same
.
Say an Indian company exports and earns $ 1 billion . Say it pays 40% tax and now has $600 million of its own forex earnings. Say that Indian company decides to bring in that $600 million into India and invest into India. OR say, some Indians company owners got forex investments from their relatives in gulf as gift or whatever.
.
Then that Indian company , as per law-drafts made by SoMoKe, DOES NOT have option to label its investment as FDI and cannot get benefits of FDI !!.
.
But if a foreign company brings $600 million , the SoMoKe insist on giving several benefits to that foreign company.
.
The benefits are
.
1. The Indian company does NOT have option of repatriation of its profits in dollars even if the company was raised by its export earnings or gifts from relatives in foreign countries.
.
2. Under various headings, not directly, SoMoKe offer income tax and capital gains tax exemption to foreign company but not Indian companies, even though they too brough forex just like the foreig company. eg SoMoKe support Mauritius treaty, Singapore treaty which make short term capital gain exempt from taxes. And there are ways to convert operating profits into short term capital gains. Plus central govt often makes many special deals which give tax exempt status to foreign company.
.
All in all, FDI isnt to get capital or forex. If so, why not offer same deals to INDIANS who bring forex thru gifts or exports or other legal means? SoMoKe's FDI implementation law-drafts show that their design is to ensure that foreigners take over a huge chunk of Indian economy.
.
Solution I propose to activists is to demand law-drafts that would create a level field between Indian companies and foreign companies. They are :-
.
1. Taxing all companies at same rate, Ending Mauritius treaty as well as Singapore treaty
.
2. No repatriation of profits in dollars or gold or silver or metals or natural resources. Repatriation of profits only by export earnings
.
3. Applying same tax rate on SEZ companies.
.
And we should also inform voters that SoMoKe are opposing law-drafts that would decrease inefficiencies of Indian factory owners, such as JurySys, RTR, wealth tax, TCP, DDMRCAM , labor or his agent should spend time and bear hassles in depositing PF , TDS etc and not the company owner etc etc.
.

Read full post →

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sun Nov 16 2014 09:07:48 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

How SoMoKe's FDI proposals give a higher level to foreign companies and put Indian companies at a lower level.
.
SoMoKe = Sonia, Modi, AK , as far as FDI goes, all three are one and the same
.
Say an Indian company exports and earns $ 1 billion . Say it pays 40% tax and now has $600 million of its own forex earnings. Say that Indian company decides to bring in that $600 million into India and invest into India. OR say, some Indians company owners got forex investments from their relatives in gulf as gift or whatever.
.
Then that Indian company , as per law-drafts made by SoMoKe, DOES NOT have option to label its investment as FDI and cannot get benefits of FDI !!.
.
But if a foreign company brings $600 million , the SoMoKe insist on giving several benefits to that foreign company.
.
The benefits are
.
1. The Indian company does NOT have option of repatriation of its profits in dollars even if the company was raised by its export earnings or gifts from relatives in foreign countries.
.
2. Under various headings, not directly, SoMoKe offer income tax and capital gains tax exemption to foreign company but not Indian companies, even though they too brough forex just like the foreig company. eg SoMoKe support Mauritius treaty, Singapore treaty which make short term capital gain exempt from taxes. And there are ways to convert operating profits into short term capital gains. Plus central govt often makes many special deals which give tax exempt status to foreign company.
.
All in all, FDI isnt to get capital or forex. If so, why not offer same deals to INDIANS who bring forex thru gifts or exports or other legal means? SoMoKe's FDI implementation law-drafts show that their design is to ensure that foreigners take over a huge chunk of Indian economy.
.
Solution I propose to activists is to demand law-drafts that would create a level field between Indian companies and foreign companies. They are :-
.
1. Taxing all companies at same rate, Ending Mauritius treaty as well as Singapore treaty
.
2. No repatriation of profits in dollars or gold or silver or metals or natural resources. Repatriation of profits only by export earnings
.
3. Applying same tax rate on SEZ companies.
.
And we should also inform voters that SoMoKe are opposing law-drafts that would decrease inefficiencies of Indian factory owners, such as JurySys, RTR, wealth tax, TCP, DDMRCAM , labor or his agent should spend time and bear hassles in depositing PF , TDS etc and not the company owner etc etc.
.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sun Nov 16 2014 11:20:06 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Some important questions in "activism for law-drafts" , and my answers
.
Following are the questions we should be asking to every activist , and following are my answers :---
.
1. What law-drafts do you propose to reduce the mess. And please give links to the FULL drafts.
.
My answer is --- law-drafts such as RTR, JurySys, TCP, wealth tax, DDMRCAM, labor or agent of labor should deposit PF, TDS , etc and many such law-drafts. The links are in rahulmehta . com /301.htm
.
====
.
2. What activities do you propose 43 crores to 83 crore voters should do, that would convince MPs who wish to listen for 40 hours a week and staff of 2 persons working 40 hours a week, that over 43 crore voters do support and demand the law-draft you propose? Time limit of 40 hours a week and staff limit is important. If one suggests activity that would require MP to spend 100 hours a week and hire a staff of 500, then such activity should be dismissed as useless.
.
My answer --- The activity I propose to 43 crore to 83 crore voters is to send orders to MPs via SMS containing URL to the law-draft .
.
====
.
3. What activities do you propose for 20 lakh activists that would ensure that some 43 crore to 83 crore voters will know about law-drafts you proposed in (1) and activities you suggested in (2) ? And please note ---- most activists have pet causes and they wont work on law-draft unless it is clear to them that their pet causes will be benefitted by the law-draft set you cited .
.
My answer : The law-drafts I have proposed cover some 100+ pet causes such as improving Military , reducing poverty, reducing corruption, reducing conversion without use of force, strengthening hindu sects, reducing higher playing field given to foreign companies, reducing inefficiencies of factory owners, expelling Bangladeshis, reducing crimes against women, improving courts, improving police, go raksha etc etc. All pet causes , except neta-pujan. IOW, there is for something all activists , unless that activist is neta-pujak or that activist has some anti-India goals. So via these 20 lakh activists, I propose to take the information on law-drafts and information on "order your MP via SMS" activity to 43 crore voters to 83 crore voters.
.
Some background to question-3 : There are only two channels to reach --- 43 crore voters --- (a) paidmedia or (b) 20 lakh activists. There is NO third way to reach 43 crore to 83 crore voters . Now SoMoKe have decided to depend on paidmedia to reach 43 crore to 83 crore voters. We dont have that option, as we support proposals like wealth tax, RTR doordarshan chairman , DDMRCAM etc.If an activist is aiming to get attention of paidmedia, then a good activist should shun him ASAP. The only other media is 20 lakh activist. So the activist must have goal to take his proposals to 20 lakh activists in India.
.
======
.
4. What activities do you propose for 20 - 2000 activists you personally know, that would ensure that they will pass the information on law-drafts and activity list to activists they know and then activists they know.
.
Reaching 43 crore to 83 crore voters is possible via 20 lakh activists or via paidmedia. I am not interested in a plan based on paidmedia. I am only interested in knowing plan which goes via 20 lakh activists. And only way to reach 20 lakh activist is via 20-2000 activists you know, who will take it to 20-2000 activists you know, and so forth.
.
So what activity list you propose to activists you know
,
My answer : My request to them is
.
(a) contest elections on law-drafts and order MP via SMS
(b) give newspaper advt on law-drafts and order MP via SMS
(c) distribute pamphlets on law-drafts and order MP via SMS
(d) make important activists as FB close friends and read - share all their status posts
(e) use FB etc to spread info law-drafts and order MP via SMS
(f) hold meeting in gardens , personal offices
(g) make YT vidoes
.
========
.
4. And finally, what activities do you do that would ensure that information on law-drafts and order your MP via SMS campaign passes to activists you know?
.
My answer : I do following activities
.
(a) contest elections on law-drafts and order MP via SMS
(b) give newspaper advt on law-drafts and order MP via SMS
(c) distribute pamphlets on law-drafts and order MP via SMS
(d) make important activists as FB close friends and read - share all their status posts
(e) use FB etc to spread info law-drafts and order MP via SMS
(f) hold meeting in gardens , personal offices
(g) make YT vidoes
.
=============================
.
IOW, activism on a cause is about taking information about solutions and means to 43 crore to 83 crore voters via activists and NOT via paidmedia.
.
And that is possible ONLY via 20 lakh activists. And no activist-A can fool or convince other activist-B to give away pet cause of activist-B and work on cause of activist-A. To fool activist-B, you would need paidmedia and thats a non-option as far as I go. So the activism program must include causes of at least 20 lakh activists !!! Any activism program that doesnt include causes dear to at least 20 lakh activists will vanish or get eventually hijacked by paidmedia, So I request activists NOT to work on a program that doesnt involve causes that appeal to at least 20 lakh activists across India. And there should be activity list which activist and voters can do, within their limited time and money. And the activity list must have activities that can create a proof to MPs who wish to listen. As per MPs who dont want to listen, there is no need to worry about them.
.
And activist must ask fellow activist to provide his list of law-drafts as well as provide list of activities he proposes for voter and fellow 20 lakh activists
.
I at RRG has given list of law-drafts and list of activities. We need to get answers from others. .

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sun Nov 16 2014 15:42:40 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

(Pls only copy-paste my posts/comments. Pls dont like\share\comment them. Like \ share disturbs me due to notification I get. If your mobile doesnt support copy-paste, then change mobile or ask your friend to copy-paste-send. But pls dont like/share/comment. )

Rahul Chimanbhai Mehta , Gandhinagar LsCandidate, Right to Recall Party:

कैसे मोदी/केजरीवाल/सोनिया की FDI के प्रस्तावों ने विदेशी कम्पनियो के लिए उच्च स्तर दिया है और भारतीय कम्पनियो को निचले स्टार पर धकेल दिया है।
.
(Pls only copy-paste my posts/comments. Pls dont like\share\comment them. Like \ share disturbs me due to notification I get. If your mobile doesnt support copy-paste, then change mobile or ask your friend to copy-paste-send. But pls dont like/share/comment. )
.
मन लीजिये एक भारतीय कंपनी निर्यात करती है और 1 अरब डॉलर कमाती है। वो भारत सरकार को 40 % टैक्स देगी और उसके पास अब 600 मिलियन डॉलर विदेशी मुद्रा की कमाई है। अब अगर वो भारत में इसका निवेश करती है तो उसको FDI के अंतर्गत मिलने वाले लाभ नहीं मिलेंगे वही अगर विदेशी कंपनी 600 मिलियन डॉलर लेके आती है तो उसको निम्नलिखित लाभ मिलेंगे
.
1 . स्वदेशी कम्पनियो को उनके कमाए हुए मुनाफे पर डॉलर नहीं मिलेगा बल्कि विदेशी कम्पनियो ऐसा लाभ मिलेगा।
.
2. विभिन शीर्षकों के अंतर्गत विदेशी कम्पनियो को आयकर और कमाए हुए लाभ पर टैक्स छूट का प्रावधान है पर स्वदेशी कम्पनियो के लिए ऐसा कोई प्रावधान नहीं। उदाहरण मॉरिशस रूट और सिंगापुर रूट से आये हुई कंपनियों को छोटी अवधि के पूंजी लाभ पर टैक्स छूट मिलती है और ऐसे कई तरीके है जिनसे बड़ी मात्र में हो रहे पूंजी लाभ को छूटी अवधि में बदला जा सके। इसके अलावा अक्सर विदेशी कम्पनियो के लिए आयकर पर छूट मिलती है।
.
तो FDI सिर्फ इसलिए नहीं है वो विदेशी मुद्रा लाये। अगर ऐसा है तो भारतीय कम्पनियो द्वारा विदेशी मुद्रा लाने पर उनको FDI के अंतर्गत क्यों नहीं माना जाता। मोदी /केजरीवाल /सोनिया की FDI नीतिया के कारन विदेशी कम्पनियो को भारत से उसकी अर्थव्यवस्था का एक बड़ा हिस्सा अपने देश ले जाने में मदद करेगी और स्वदेशी कम्पनियो को बर्बाद।
.
समाधान।
.
1. सारी कम्पनियो पर एक तरह का टैक्स लगाया जाये। मॉरिशस और सिंगापुर ट्रीटी को रद्द किया जाये।
.
2 . भारत सरकार किदेशी कंपनियों के कमाए हुए मुनाफे पर डॉलर/ सोने /या अन्य खनिज का भुगतान नहीं करे। तथा केवल निर्यात से लए गए डॉलर का ही भुगतान करे।
.
3. सेज़ कम्पनियो पर समान टैक्स लगाये।
.
====================================
.
हमे ज्यादा से ज्यादा नागरिको को यह बताना चाहिए की मोदी/ सोनिया / केजरीवाल स्वदेशी कंपनियों की बेहतरी के लिए जरुरी कानून जैसे की जूरी सिस्टम , राइट टू रिकॉल /वेल्थ टैक्स / टी सी पी / डी डी आर सी एम / आदि का विरोध आकर रहे है और विदेशि कम्पनियो के लिए रेड कारपेट बिछा रहे है।

Read full post →

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sun Nov 16 2014 17:19:45 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

More on non-80G-activism aka true activism
.
Pls see my yesterday's post on activism. This is in continuation with that.
.
Activism can be paid 80G-activism like NGO paying for activism or fully non-80G-activism. This is about non-80G-activism ONLY.
.
There are 83 crore voters in India, of which say 20 lakh to 1 crore are willing to give say 4 hours a week of time and some 1% of their money for their pet cause. The remaining 83 crore minus those 1 crore to 20 lakhs wont even do that. All they will can do is give vote and/or moral support, which is a lot. But they wont give time or money for their cause, at least not in first round. And all over world, situation is more or less the same. So this isnt the reason why India is lagging.
.
Now some activists may be willing to give lot more time and lot more % of their incomes.
.
The activist will need convey their cause to 84 crore voters, or at least 43 crores or at least 10 crore. AFAIS, there are ONLY two ways.
.
1. paidmedia
.
2. ask 20 lakh activists to spread information etc on your causes.
.
3. or dead end There is no third way.
.
I am not interested in paidmedia part. So I will skip it.
.
Now

.

Pawan Jury BhilwaraRj

Sun Nov 16 2014 18:32:05 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

as I inform people about jury system, 50% peoples support it. rest 50% refused it or put long arguments against jury trial.
why ?
as I realised they cant digest such system, where commons give verdict. even I inform that US follow jury system, they fail to feel real scenario.
solution : I recommend to watch holywood movie 'EXORCISM OF EMILY ROSE' to feel what does jury trial means.
movie based on a real incedent and perfectly define jury trial.
.
as indian example I suggest to read short story 'पञ्च परमेश्वर' written by munshi premchand.
story prove that jury trial situated in british india, but replaced by 'panchayat raj' after independent.
story tells about two close friends A & B, where A had a civil claim on B. when all villagers come to panchayat, the moment come to choose 'PANCH' as jury. PANCH choosen randomly by villagers for particular case.
.
In indian prospects, an intresting drama by zury, conducted in 'एक रुका हुआ फैसला' in 1984.
in short : a jury of 12 citizens club in a room to make decesion abt 19 years old accused, who has charged to his father's murder. as per orders, they got merely 6 hours (11am-5 pm) to take their decision.
====

It should be learn, that laws rule contry, but its judiciary, which rule the laws.
every law has a penal and prison provision. if anyone break the law, here court comes to send him behind the bars. if we dont have fast and non biased courts, laws will be useless. if any country bad laws abt judiciary, whole system and laws will be corrupt.
In india we have 3 crores pending cases in courts. every case engage 2 individuals, some eye witness, IO. besides it every person have a family. means 3×10 = 30 carores citizens are hanging in indian courts. we have only 15000 judges, where china has 2 lacs.
.
solution : we must move to jury system to fix the mess. judge system provide a neta-police-judge nexus. due to judge system, our courts are slow and corrupt, it must be replaced by jury system by now.
please note that SoMoKe (soniya-modi-kejriwal) are opposing to bring jury system in india. so we 'the people' must take initiative to send orders to our MP.
.
please send this order to your MP via SMS.
'I order you to print mentioned law draft in gazette.
tinyurl . com/jurysys
_________________

right to recall @ pk

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Mon Nov 17 2014 03:00:29 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Existing PSU like Railways or city utilities use following model
.
1. The heads , be elected or appointed, they arent recallable by voters.
.
2 The staff is under judges and NOT under Juries.
.
====
.
The model we at Right to Recall Group propose/support is RTR-Jury model as
.
1. The heads , be elected or appointed, should be made recallable by specific RTR-draft
.
2 The staff is under Juries and NOT under judges.
.
=====
.
The model SoMoKe propose/support is private-foreign model aka
.
1. Sell PSU such as Railways etc and even municipal services to foreign or Indian private companies.
.
2 The staff will be under private management with only rudimentary control by judges
.
======
.
Between existing and RTR-Jury SoMoKe-andbhagats and CoBhApSwaRa-workers support existing model as recall and Juries in their opinions are bad.
.
Between existing and private-foreign model , SoMoKe-andbhagats and CoBhApSwaRa-workers support private-foreign model !!!
.
And SoMoKe-andbhagats and CoBhApSwaRa-worker will only compare existing and private-foreign model . They never dare to compare these two models with RTR-Jury model.

Pawan Jury BhilwaraRj

Tue Nov 18 2014 09:55:55 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

संत रामपाल को कोर्ट अब तक 42 समन भेज चुका है, किन्तु संत श्री हाज़िर नहीं हुए है । हाईकोर्ट ने स्वत: संज्ञान लेते हुए संत को अदालत में हाज़िर होने का आदेश दिया है । संत कह रहे है कि वे बीमार है, किन्तु कोर्ट और सरकार को उन्हें हाज़िर करने पर तुली हुयी है ।
.
पुलिस और सेना के 35000 जवानो ने आश्रम पर घेरा डाल रखा है, जबकि 1 लाख भक्त आश्रम की दीवार बन कर खड़े हुए है । पुलिस द्वारा आसूं गैस के गोले और पानी की बौछारे की जा रही है, जबकि भक्तो द्वारा पेट्रोल बम और पथराव किया जा रहा है । भक्तो की अगली पंक्ति में महिलाओं ने मोर्चा संभाला हुआ है, जिस से पुलिस आगे नही बढ़ पा रही है ।
बताया जा रहा है कि संत श्री के पास 20 हजार हथियारबंद निजी सुरक्षा गार्ड है, जो कि किसी भी हद तक संघर्ष करने को उतारू है ।
ज्ञातव्य है कि, संत रामपाल ने विधानसभा चुनाव में बीजेपी को वोट करने के लिए अपील जारी की थी ।
2006 में भी संत रामपाल को बरनाला आश्रम से गिरफ्तार करने में प्रशासन को काफी मशक्कत करनी पड़ी थी ।
.
निम्न स्थितियों पर विचार करे :
1. यदि भारत में हर व्यक्ति को हथियार रखने की छूट हो, तो यह बवाल क्या रूप ले सकता है ?
2. मान लीजिये कि 1 लाख में से 50 हजार के पास बंदूके, राइफल, रोकेट लांचर और ऐ के 47 है, तो मामले को कैसे सुलझाया जा सकेगा ।
3. यदि बरनाला का पुलिस प्रमुख, स्वत:संज्ञान लेने वाला जज और मुख्यमंत्री प्रजा अधीन हो ।
4. यदि हरियाणा में ज्यूरी सिस्टम हो ।
____________________

प्रजा अधीन राजा @ pk

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Tue Nov 18 2014 14:31:30 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

I request all recallists in west Bengal to join this party

Chandra Kumar Bose:

People of West Bengal are known to be patient & long suffering when it comes to exploitation by political parties! We have seen the days of Congress rule under the leadership of Siddhartha Shankar Ray. Youth suspected to be Naxalites- were butchered by the police with the instruction of their political bosses. Then there were police atrocities during Left Front rule culminating with the violence at Nandigram. The Left Front had to relinquish power due to Nandigram. However, what we find now is mindboggling & sends a shiver down the spine of even the courageous!
The TMC Party & Government have shattered all previous records by emerging as the most violent entity in the political spectrum. The People of West Bengal must retrospect & do some serious thinking regarding the performance of their beloved CM & her Government!
In the crime charts the TMC Party & Government have topped the list in molestation, rape & murder. Any person who challenges the Government is liable to be inflicted with any or all of the above. Park Street to Katua to Kamduni- the administration has miserably failed to nab & punish the culprits. It appears if one registers with the TMC- one gets a license to steal, rape & murder under the protection of the TMC Party & Government.
What is happening in Birbhum, Parui & Burdwan is like a story from the wild! The administration is a silent spectator to all the terror & violence unleashed by the TMC. The Police administration does not exist. In Parui it is controlled by a Trinamool goon- Anubrata Mandal. In Jadavpur the official MP- Sugata Bose is absconding! Wed Nov 19 2014 18:55:12 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

19 नवम्बर, कल पेड एनडीटीवी पर प्राइम टाइम के दौरान एक साधू और साध्वी का पूर्व नियोजित साक्षात्कार 'घटनास्थल' से प्रसारित किया गया । घटनास्थल से मायने एक तीन दिवसीय धरने के मंच से है, जहाँ आरामदेह गद्दों पर 25-30 साधु साफ़ एवं करीने से इस्तरी किये हुए एक जैसे चटख रंग के केसरिया वस्त्र पहने मंचासीन थे । अग्रिम पंक्ति में नेतृत्व कर्ता साधु महाराज शुभ्र और धवल वस्त्रो में विराजमान थे, जिनका कि साक्षात्कार प्रसारित किया गया ।
.
मांग - ये गिरोह मांग कर रहा है कि कर्नाटक में अंधश्रद्धा निर्मूलन क़ानून पारित किया जाए । परन्तु झमेला यह है कि इनका मानना है ज्योतिष और वास्तुशास्त्र भी अंधश्रद्धा है, और इनसे सम्बंधित प्रकाशनों और व्यवहार को निषेध किया जाना चाहिए ।
.
एंकर रविश कुमार इन्हें बार बार यह याद दिला रहे थे कि महाराष्ट्र में जो क़ानून पास हुआ, वो जादू टोना निषेध तक ही सिमट कर रह गया जबकि आन्दोलन अंधश्रद्धा निर्मूलन के लिए किया गया था । प्रकरांतर में नरेन्द्र दाभोलकर का भी स्मरण किया गया ।
ज्ञातव्य है कि डॉ दाभोलकर अमेरिका से प्राप्त चंदे से अन्धश्रधा निर्मूलन समिति का कारोबार करते थे, जिनकी हत्या पिछले वर्ष गोली मारकर कर दी गयी थी । यह संस्था भारत में हिन्दू धर्म को उखाड़ने के लिए कुछ कानूनों की मांग पिछले 20 वर्षो से कर रही है ।
.
ध्वनित कुछ इस तरह हो रहा था कि रामपाल जैसे प्रकरणों का समाधान इस अंधश्रद्धा निर्मूलन क़ानून में निहित है । देश में बढ़ते स्वयंभू संतो, साधूओ, बाबाओं और उनके द्वारा बनाए जा रहे अड्डो नुमा आश्रमों को नियमित करने के लिए जूरी सिस्टम, राईट टू रिकाल तथा हिन्दू देवालय अधिनियम जैसे कानूनों की जरूरत है ताकि इन पर लोकमत का अंकुश लगाया जा सके। यदि नागरिक इन कानूनों की मांग अपने सांसदों से नहीं करते है तो मिशनरीज़ और MNCs को पेड मिडिया के माध्यम से यह भ्रम फैलाने का अच्छा अवसर मिलेगा कि बांसुरी की कर्कश ध्वनि से बचने का उपाय यह है कि बांस के व्यापार पर ही रोक लगा दी जाए ।

The trials on Sant Rampal , Asaram Bapu etc and ALL trials in India should be done by Jury and NOT by judges. I never had faith in judges, since I was kid. And now it is OFFICIALLY known and accepted by most that most judges are corrupt, nepotic, always looking for transfers, promotions, trying to get relatives placed etc etc.
.
Along with Jury Trials, we also need Right to Recall judges so that voters can get corrupt , nepotic judges expelled and replace them with less corrupt individuals.
.
On one hand murtipujal akanyaya-murti-pujak aka judge-worshippers say that the judges are non-corrupt. And in next line, they will oppose Right to recall judges !! Well, if judges are non-corrupt and non-nepotic, then why should people expel judges ? Basically, they know that most judges are corrupt and nepotic and they know that if RTR judges come, then most judges will not only get expelled but will also land in jail once Jury takes their narco-test on cases of their corruption and NEPOTISM.
.
It isnt just case of Baba Rampal or Asaram Bapu. I am speaking for all 3 crore pending cases and some 30 lakh cases that come every year. All cases should be decided by Jury of 12 , 25, 50, 100, 200, 500, 1000 or 1500 as may be the case and the strength of the accused. And the appeal too should be decided by the Jurors at State/National level only and not the judges.
.
More about JurySys is in chap-21, chap-22 of rahulmehta .com/301.htm .
.
How can YOU work bring Jury System and Right to Recall judges in India?
.
You can send following two orders to MPs via SMS
.
Dear MP , I order you to print Right to Recall District judge draft in Gazette, given at tinyurl .com/RtrDistJudge .
.
Dear MP, I order you to print Jury System in Lower Court draft in Gazette given at tinyurl .com/JurySys
.

Right to Recall:

संत रामपाल , आसाराम बापू और भारत के सभी केस के परिक्षण जूरी द्वारा किये जाने चाहिए, न की भ्रस्ट जजो द्वारा। और यह आधिकारिक तोर पर सब जानते है की जज भ्रस्ट होते है और हमेशा पदोनत्ति , ट्रांसफर की सोचते है और अपने रिश्तोदारो को नौकरी पर लगवाते है।
.
जूरी सिस्टम के साथ साथ हमे राइट टू रिकॉल जज की भी आवशयकता है ताकि आम नागरिक इन भ्रस्ट जजो को नौकरी से निकल सके और ईमानदार जजो की इनकी जगह रख सके।
.
राइट टू रिकॉल जज आने पर हम भ्रस्ट जजो को हटा पाएंगे और जूरी सिस्टम की मदद से उनके किये गए भ्रस्टाचार और भाई भतीजावाद पर उनको सजा भी मिल पायेगी।
.
यह सिर्फ संत रामपाल और आसाराम की बात नहीं है बल्कि लंबित पड़े 3 करोड़ मामलो की बात है और हर साल लगभग 30 लाख नए मामले आते है। सभी मामलों में 12 ,25 ,50 ,100 ,500 ,1000,1500 , की जूरी द्वारा निर्णय लिया जाना चाहिए। जूरी की संख्या आरोपी की ताकत पर निर्भर करेगी। और राष्ट्रीय / राज्य स्तर पर अपील भी जूरी द्वारा ही ली जनि चाहिए न की जजो द्वारा।
,
जूरी सिस्टम के बारे में अभिक जानकारी के लिए देखे chap-21, chap-22 of rahulmehta.com/301.htm .
.
हम कैसे जूरी सिस्टम भारत में ल सकते है ?
.
हमे SMS के माध्यम से सांसदों को आर्डर भेजने होंगे।
.1. पहला SMS
.
प्रिय संसद , में आपको आर्डर देता हूँ की आप भारत के राजपत्र में राइट टू रिकॉल डिस्ट्रिक्ट जज का कानून डाले। जिसका यूआरएल है .tinyurl.com/RtrDistJudge .

.
2. दूसरा SMS
.
प्रिय संसद में आपको आर्डर देता हूँ की निचली अदालतों के मसौदे में जूरी सिस्टम मुद्रित किया जाये। कानून का यूआरएल है tinyurl.com/JurySys

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Sat Nov 22 2014 07:19:51 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Due to govt control over temples, the temples are becoming hell holes of corruption and mistreatment. And so Sanatani temples are losing followers by lakhs and crores. And these Hindus are becoming followers of several other Sant such as Sant Asaram Bapu, Sant Rampal etc. And some Hindus are becoming followers of Missionaries. A huge number of their followers are SC, ST and OBC Hindus.
.
I didnt know anything ANYTHING about Sant Rampal some 30 days back. But yesterday, I came to know that there are 100s of SC, OBC villages in Bihar where almost every family is follower of Sant Rampal !! A friend of mine, Aditya Anand travels widely across Indian villages across villages across states , and he said that Sant Rampal's following is in 10s of lakhs across India if not crores. And most of his followers are SC, ST and OBCs.
.
Sant Rampal is Kabirpathi. Kabirpath is growing by leaps and bounds. As per Kabirpanth thought, Sant Kabir is the main God in the universe !!! The whole universe was created by Sant Kabir . Sant Kabir had taken birth in form of Vasishtha , then Ved Vyas etc and then finally he took birth as Sant Kabir himself. All these thoughts were documents by Sant Abhilash Das some 30-60 years back. Sant Abhilash Das passed away some 5-7 years back, and his books form dominant litreture in Kabirpanthes.
.
Kabirpath has several Sahib aka Guru . Most Guru in Kabirpath dont abuse Saints of others. They only confine to criticism of their views in legitimate language. But Sant Rampal decided to use abusive words for many Saints, particularly Swamy Dayanand Saraswati. The paidmedia gave him hug coverage and so his following increased even more. And this created series of disputes and rukus between followers of Sant Rampal and followers of Swamy Dayanand Saraswati.
.
Now the disputes are there. And disputes must be resolved, and if force needs to be used, then force needs to be used. The question is how and how should decide the use of force, who should decide amount of force to be used and who should decide direction of use of force.
.
The mess is increasing because followers on both side want to use private force , aggresive gherao and force administered by corrupt and nepotic judges. I oppose use of private force as well as gherao. These two methods only weaken the nation and benefit the enemy such as Missionaries, naxals. etc.
.
And I also oppose force administered by corrupt , nepotic judges because most judges are corrupt, nepotic, career oriented, more looking for favorable transfers and promotion, and also scared of taking stand against corrupt bosses in High Court and Supreme Court. If we use force administered by judges, then judges will sell out to highest bidders and give judgment as decided by highest bidders. The opponents of Sant Rampal can always manage to bribe judges and crush him and his ashram using force. But then chances are high that his followers will turn to naxals or missionaries.
.
The solution I propose is Jury Trials on all disputes --- starting with use of abusive language to land dispute in ashram to use of gherao by his opponents. The Jurors arent corrupt,are not nepotic, dont have have desire for promotion etc , are not afraid of transfers etc , and so will give mininal required punishments in RIGHT directions, and would acquit the person if no punishments are needed. This will ensure that disputes dont escalate. Otherwise, dispute will escalates, and only beneficiaies will be naxals in first round, and then Missiionaries and assorted foreigners in the second round.
